# Agents · D · part 7

914 entries, db-mysql to deadline-watcher. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **db-mysql**: Use this agent for MySQL work: schema and index design around InnoDB's clustered-index storage, query tuning, execution plans, transactions and gap locking, replication topology and lag, and migration safety on large tables. ([dppereyra/dotfiles](https://github.com/dppereyra/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/src/configs/.copilot/agents/db-mysql.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **db-neo4j-expert**: Expert in Neo4j 5.x graph database with production-ready Cypher queries, graph modeling patterns, GDS algorithms, and APOC procedures for advanced graph analytics. ([andisab/swe-marketplace](https://github.com/andisab/swe-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/databases/agents/db-neo4j-expert.md) · ★23)
- **db-nosql-modeling**: Design NoSQL data models — document, key-value, wide-column, graph, time-series — with access pattern analysis, consistency requirements, and schema evolution. ([quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk](https://github.com/quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/platform/adapters/claude/agents/db-db-nosql-modeling.md))
- **db-onboarding-coach**: Use this agent when a learner needs guidance starting or navigating the database-as-code-kit curriculum. Typical triggers include a new learner asking "where do I start if I know nothing about databases", an experienced engineer wanting to skip to advanced modules and needing a recommended path, and someone asking what a specific lesson covers before deciding whether to read it. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([leduykhuong-daniel/OSS-database-as-code-kit](https://github.com/leduykhuong-daniel/OSS-database-as-code-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/db-onboarding-coach.md))
- **db-operations-reviewer**: Review database migrations, query changes, transaction boundaries, RLS/tenant isolation, locking, indexes, backfills, and production rollout safety. ([kidboy-man/vibe-engineering](https://github.com/kidboy-man/vibe-engineering/blob/HEAD/agents/kits/claude_code/templates/claude/agents/db-operations-reviewer.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **db-operator**: Operatore database (db_access, opt-in). Esegue indagini in più passi sui PostgreSQL degli ambienti del registro via tools/db/db_access.py con le credenziali del file env configurato, partendo anche da servizio, schema o tabella (locate, tables, describe). Legge con query validate e read-only lato server; gli ambienti in read_only_envs non si scrivono mai; la scrittura e' spenta per default (db_access.write.enabled) e, se accesa, l'agente si ferma al dry-run e restituisce un gate JSON R.14 alla sessione principale, mai --commit. Gated su db_access.enabled. ([Almusweb/wiki-factory](https://github.com/Almusweb/wiki-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/db-operator.md))
- **db-ops**: Agent that handles production DB configuration, migration procedures, backup/restore, and destructive change risk assessment. Used in the following situations: - Launched on Standard/Full plans of the Operations flow - When asked to "design DB operations" or "create migration procedures" Prerequisite: ARCHITECTURE.md must have a data model defined ([kirin0198/aphelion-agents](https://github.com/kirin0198/aphelion-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/db-ops.md))
- **db-optimizer**: Audits repo layer for N+1, missing indexes, offset pagination, missing model_validate. Run before shipping any feature with DB queries. ([Shreyas1015/create-rigel](https://github.com/Shreyas1015/create-rigel/blob/HEAD/templates/fastapi/.claude/agents/db-optimizer.md) · ★7)

- **db-performance-auditor**: Audits a codebase's data-access layer end to end for performance in any stack - ORM query patterns (N+1, projections, tracking, eager/lazy loading), indexing gaps, ORM and connection-pool configuration, retry/resiliency, and bulk-operation opportunities - across EF Core, Dapper, Prisma, TypeORM, Drizzle, SQLAlchemy, Django ORM, Hibernate/JPA, Spring Data, GORM, and raw SQL. Produces a ranked report with fixes. Use when the user wants a database or ORM performance review of a repo, "why is my data layer slow", "audit our Prisma/EF/Hibernate usage", or a pre-release DB performance pass. - **ddd-reviewer**: DDD 準拠レビュー、コードが DDD の原則に従っているかの確認、ドメインモデルの品質へのフィードバックをユーザーが求めたときに、このエージェントを使用する。 常にエキスパートレベルの厳格なレビュー基準を適用する。例: &lt;example&gt; Context: ユーザーがドメインモデルの DDD 準拠チェックを求めている user: "このドメインモデルがDDDに準拠しているかレビューして" assistant: "ddd-reviewer エージェントでDDD準拠度を厳密にレビューする。" &lt;commentary&gt; 明示的な DDD レビューの要求。包括的な準拠チェックのために ddd-reviewer を起動する。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: ユーザーが新しい集約を実装し、フィードバックを求めている user: "新しく作った集約のコードを見てほしい" assistant: "ddd-reviewer エージェントで集約の設計品質をレビューする。" &lt;commentary&gt; 新しい集約のコードのレビューが必要。DDD パターンを検証するために ddd-reviewer を起動する。 &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: ユーザーが DDD に正しく従えているか確信が持て… ([dskst/ddd-skills](https://github.com/dskst/ddd-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ddd-reviewer.md) · ★1)
